Synon Biomed — frontend third-party notices
===============================================

Third-party packages retain their respective licenses and copyrights.
These terms are not replaced by the root AGPL or a Synon commercial agreement.
Code is bundled/minified for browser use; upstream package implementations are not edited.

This notice covers packages in the browser bundle and the copied RDKit runtime.
Other components and optional scientific runtimes have separate notices in this directory.

    means Covered Software of a particular Contributor.

    means Source Code Form to which the initial Contributor has attached
    the notice in Exhibit A, the Executable Form of such Source Code

    including portions thereof.

        in Exhibit B to the Covered Software; or

        version 1.1 or earlier of the License, but not also under the
        terms of a Secondary License.

    means any form of the work other than Source Code Form.

1.7. "Larger Work"
    means a work that combines Covered Software with other material, in
    a separate file or files, that is not Covered Software.

1.8. "License"
    means this document.

1.9. "Licensable"
    means having the right to grant, to the maximum extent possible,
    whether at the time of the initial grant or subsequently, any and
    all of the rights conveyed by this License.

1.10. "Modifications"
    means any of the following:

    (a) any file in Source Code Form that results from an addition to,
        deletion from, or modification of the contents of Covered
        Software; or

    (b) any new file in Source Code Form that contains any Covered
        Software.

    means any patent claim(s), including without limitation, method,

1.14. "You" (or "Your")
    means an individual or a legal entity exercising rights under this

import fs from 'fs';

import { fileURLToPath } from 'url';
import { dirname } from 'path';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = dirname(__filename);

const year = (new Date()).getFullYear();

const license = `Copyright (c) 2016-${year}, The Cytoscape Consortium.

Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy of
this software and associated documentation files (the “Software”), to deal in
the Software without restriction, including without limitation the rights to
use, copy, modify, merge, publish, distribute, sublicense, and/or sell copies
of the Software, and to permit persons to whom the Software is furnished to do

copies or substantial portions of the Software.
